    hidden = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    title = "DUMMY"
    desc = "DUMMY"
    picture = TRADE_GOODS_FURS_FISH_AND_SALT_eventPicture

    trigger = {
        tag = PLB
    }

    immediate = {
        if = { limit = { tag = YUG has_reform = peasants_republic } override_country_name = PLB_YUG_PEASANTS_SFRY }
        if = { limit = { tag = BLR has_reform = monarchy_mechanic NOT = { government_rank = 2 } } override_country_name = PLB_BLR_GRAND_DUCHY_GRAND_DUCHY }
    }

    option = {
        name = dynamic_names_02_slavic_formables_00_vanilla.695.a
    }
}

    hidden = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    title = "DUMMY"
    desc = "DUMMY"
    picture = TRADE_GOODS_FURS_FISH_AND_SALT_eventPicture

    trigger = {
        tag = STA
    }

    immediate = {
        if = { limit = { tag = YUG has_reform = peasants_republic } override_country_name = STA_YUG_PEASANTS_SFRY }
        if = { limit = { tag = BLR has_reform = monarchy_mechanic NOT = { government_rank = 2 } } override_country_name = STA_BLR_GRAND_DUCHY_GRAND_DUCHY }
    }

    option = {
        name = dynamic_names_02_slavic_formables_00_vanilla.818.a
    }
}
